The 2026-06-20 Plan 09 UI graph-tail ordering slice makes the default Forward+ and Deferred 3D pipeline assets execute scene overlay/debug work before screen-space UI. Their `RenderPhase` / `RenderPassStage` order now places `Ui` after `Overlay` and `Debug`, and the compiled graph pass order places `overlay-gizmo` before terminal `runtime-ui`. `pipeline_overlay_order.rs` covers this as a compile contract for both default 3D pipelines. The same validation lane also updated the plugin post-process test fixture so `FINAL_COMPOSITED` is authored as a graph texture rather than an external resource when builtin FXAA reads terminal AA input.

The focused product follow-up keeps the runtime executor order aligned with each compiled pipeline instead of using a fixed late-stage list. Default 3D pipelines therefore report `last_ui_graph_pass_order = "postprocess-overlay-ui"` while Core2D keeps the legacy `postprocess-ui-overlay` order. `runtime_ui_graph_pass_order(...)` is now the shared stats helper for those two valid sequences. The `render_product_ui` WGPU coverage includes default 3D pass-order stats, dynamic-resolution primary-surface and direct-texture targets where UI stays on the unscaled presentation extent, and a pixel product scene that draws dense green `overlay-gizmo` lines under a central cyan runtime UI quad and asserts the UI pixels dominate the final capture.

The 2026-06-20 Plan 09 transparent mixed-submission follow-up changes the 3D `mesh.transparent` executor path when transparent 3D sprites are present. `RenderPassGpuExecutionContext::record_mesh_stage_to_resources(...)` now selects `BaseScenePass::record_transparent_mixed_with_attachment_ops(...)` for `Transparent3d` plus sprite phase items, and the pass records Mesh and Sprite draws in the same WGPU render pass according to the final `u64` sort key. The mesh command replayer invalidates cached state after Sprite draws so the next Mesh command rebinds pipeline, material, GPU scene, and geometry as needed. Default Forward+ and Deferred transparent mesh stages provide the Sprite renderer to this path even when the 2D Sprite graph feature is absent; the actual mixed draw still gates on transparent 3D Sprite phase items. `m4_behavior_layers/transparent3d.rs::transparent3d_product_interleaves_mesh_and_sprite_pixels_by_phase_sort_key` validates the WGPU product result by sampling a green transparent Sprite behind a red transparent Mesh in the final capture. `m4_behavior_layers/transparent3d.rs::transparent3d_product_treats_world_space_ui_sprite_as_transparent_member` covers the Plan 09 world-space UI rule by giving a transparent 3D Sprite a high `ui_z_index` and proving it still stays in the transparent 3D queue, sorts by depth before the nearer Mesh, and blends in the final capture. RenderDoc capture remains the next acceptance step for this slice; this environment currently has no running RenderDoc MCP Bridge instance, so WGPU's graphics-debugger capture hook would only forward/no-op without a real `.rdc` artifact.

The texture target metadata, residency, and writeback follow-ups extend that same path: valid render-target RGBA8 texture assets now report `Texture` plus the descriptor extent, unresolved/non-render-target/non-renderable-format textures still fail before stats advance, linear `rgba8unorm` texture targets receive a renderer-owned conversion pass, and `RenderCameraTargetWritebackReport` publishes copied/converted/skipped/blocked status plus marker evidence under `RenderStats.last_camera_target_writeback` and `render.camera.target.writeback.*` diagnostics. The 2026-06-07 graph-import follow-ups record the parallel `RenderCameraTargetGraphImportReport`: readiness preflight marks `rgba8unorm_srgb` texture targets as `ReadyForDirectImport` with zero direct-import count, graph execution upgrades successful frames to `DirectImported` with one direct-import count, linear `rgba8unorm` targets remain `RequiresConversionWriteback`, and unsupported descriptors remain blocked under `RenderStats.last_camera_target_graph_import` plus `render.camera.target.graph_import.*` diagnostics. The capture-source follow-up records the readback result as `RenderCaptureReport`: primary/headless frames capture from framework offscreen color, sRGB texture targets capture from the direct graph-imported target, and linear texture targets capture from the converted prepared target rather than stale framework final color. `RenderStats.last_capture_report` and `render.capture.*` diagnostics expose only target/source/status/extent metadata. This is descriptor-looped offscreen submit, not Bevy-wide image/texture-view target parity or complete Base/Overlay composition, but eligible sRGB texture targets still receive the graph final output directly instead of a post-graph final-color copy. The 2026-06-19 layered texture-target guard extends the earlier Base-red-clear preservation check with a real green mesh visible only to an Overlay camera: the focused WGPU product test captures the same sRGB texture through direct graph import, verifies `DirectImported`/`SkippedDirectImport`, and counts both retained red base pixels and green overlay pixels in the final texture. The dynamic-resolution terminal-AA follow-up also fixes the graph descriptor contract: `FINAL_COMPOSITED` is sized to the effective view/presentation extent, matching `UPSCALED`, because terminal FXAA/SMAA reads it after output transfer/upscale rather than at the scaled internal render extent.

The 2026-06-03 dynamic-resolution history-size follow-up passed scoped formatting, conflict-marker, and `git diff --check` scans over touched render/docs files, then passed `cargo check -p zircon_runtime --lib --locked --jobs 1 --target-dir F:\cargo-targets\zircon-render-main-chain-history --message-format short --color never` with existing warnings. The focused `cargo test -p zircon_runtime --lib render_framework_invalidates_history_when_dynamic_render_size_changes --locked --jobs 1 --target-dir F:\cargo-targets\zircon-render-main-chain-history --message-format short --color never` attempt was blocked before test execution by unrelated active `static_manifest_contracts` test harness compile errors (`E0364` private re-exports and `E0282` inference errors). During validation, two unrelated UI test-harness compile blockers were mechanically unblocked: `dispatch_navigation_input(...)` route-policy annotation now ends the mutable borrow before reading route policy, and the untracked slider renderer test helper calls `state.disabled()` consistently.

The 2026-06-02 post-process graph record-owner slice used the same target dir: `stage_execution_records_post_process_graph_through_record_owner` passed 1 test, `render_product_post_process` passed 10 tests, `render_framework_stats_report_executed_product_postprocess_nodes` passed 1 test, and `graph_execution` passed 35 tests.

The 2026-06-17 Plan 01 `RgResourceResolver` follow-up starts the submit-side resolver hard cutover at the post-process boundary. `RenderPassExecutionContext::require_texture_view_by_name(...)` now validates the current compiled pass resource declaration before resolving the physical WGPU texture view, and product postprocess required-resource validation plus output-transfer/bloom/uber input selection use resolver-backed name access instead of copied context resource rows. Follow-up GPU slices carry the resolver into `RenderPassGpuExecutionContext`, move shared physical lookup helpers into `gpu/resource_lookup.rs`, split deferred scene bridge methods into `gpu/deferred.rs`, split sprite/preview-sky/UI/overlay bridge methods into `gpu/surface.rs`, and migrate depth-prepass, deferred G-buffer, deferred lighting, mesh-stage, TAA reactive-mask mesh, scene-surface, particle transparent/velocity, SSR-specific bridge, and root postprocess bridge texture/buffer lookups. The particles plugin executor now enters that runtime bridge directly instead of first touching `gpu.resources` for texture-view prechecks. The SSR slice also declares `light-list` as an external buffer read on resolve, reflection-pyramid, coarse-pyramid, and specular-occlusion descriptor passes so the shared post-process bind group is covered by compiled graph access. The root postprocess slice adds `post_process/{effects,computed_resources,temporal,terminal}.rs`, keeps root `gpu/post_process.rs` at 490 lines, and leaves no direct execution-resource lookup in the postprocess subtree outside `gpu/resource_lookup.rs`. The HZB/shadow/velocity follow-up also moves HZB previous-history reads, shadow-atlas writes, and object-velocity attachments through resolver helpers; HZB keeps its first-frame fallback texture but now still requires the compiled pass to declare the history read. Raw `RenderGraphExecutionResources` texture/buffer lookup methods are narrowed to graph-execution scope. The latest isolated runtime `core-min` check for the resolver target dir passes with the existing warning set after a shared-target fingerprint write failure and cold-target timeout; the package-scoped particles plugin check still stops before compilation because `zircon_plugins/Cargo.lock` would need an update under `--locked`. Remaining direct lookup calls are the intentional `gpu/resource_lookup.rs` helper fallback/internal calls.

The 2026-06-02 M7 effect-stack parameter slice added focused regressions for `render_product_post_process_effect_stack_runs_before_final_composite_when_authored`, `render_product_post_process_extended_effect_stack_settings_enable_product_node`, `render_framework_stats_report_effect_stack_product_node_when_authored`, and the product-postprocess executor registry coverage for `post.effect-stack`. The follow-up M7 volume-stack slice added `render_framework_stats_report_volume_effect_stack_product_node_when_authored` and pre-hard-cut unit tests for priority/layer/local-blend and extended-effect resolution; those old stack tests are superseded by `volume_evaluator.rs` after the 2026-06-15 PP-M2-S3 hard cut. The renderer consumption slice added `effect_stack_settings_are_encoded_into_post_process_params` to cover the uniform encoding consumed by `post_process.wgsl`; the camera-depth slice extended that bridge with near/far/projection params and added `orthographic_camera_depth_params_disable_perspective_linearization`. The report slice added `effect_stack_report_records_active_approximated_and_missing_resources` and extended the direct/volume bridge tests to assert `last_post_process_effect_stack_report` for active families, approximation labels, and missing LUT/SSR normal resources. The authored-LUT binding slice added `effect_stack_report_treats_authored_lut_as_renderer_bound_resource` plus enabled/disabled LUT resource-id filters in `resource_streamer_ensure_scene_resources.rs`, proving submit-resolved LUT handles drive renderer resource preparation and no longer count as approximation-backed. The LUT readiness/request slice added `color_lookup_intensity_requests_lut_even_without_texture_handle`, `effect_stack_lut_texture_request_tracks_enabled_lut_without_handle`, and `render.post_process.lut.*` diagnostics. The LUT shape-contract follow-up added `RenderColorLookupTextureLayout`, 2D-strip validation, explicit 3D shape classification, prepared texture descriptor retention, and shape diagnostic rows for `texture_2d_strip_ready_count`, `texture_3d_request_count`, and `unsupported_shape_count`. The 3D binding follow-up added dedicated post-process LUT texture resources, binding 12, a 2x2x2 identity cube fallback, shader parsing/sampling regressions for the 3D branch, and renamed the resource-streamer status test to `effect_stack_lut_texture_status_accepts_3d_lut_for_texture_3d_binding`. The 3D sampling follow-up added binding 13, a clamp/linear sampler, and a shader contract requiring `textureSampleLevel(...)` for the 3D branch while leaving 2D strip on `textureLoad(...)`. The SSR temporal diagnostics follow-up added `effect_stack_report_treats_bound_ssr_normal_as_available_but_keeps_temporal_history_gap`, `effect_stack_report_records_ssr_temporal_velocity_stage_gaps`, `effect_stack_report_clears_ssr_temporal_labels_when_history_and_velocity_chain_are_ready`, and `effect_stack_resource_status_detects_graph_bound_ssr_temporal_history_without_prepass`. The `.cube` asset-ingress follow-up added `zircon.builtin.texture.cube_lut`, linear D3 RGBA8 `TextureAsset` output, parser metadata compatibility, explicit 1D shaper rejection, and importer capability coverage. Focused validation used `CARGO_TARGET_DIR=E:\cargo-targets\zircon-render-main-chain`: `cargo test -p zircon_runtime --lib post_process_shader_samples_bound_effect_lut_texture_3d --locked --jobs 1 --target-dir E:\cargo-targets\zircon-render-main-chain --message-format short --color never` passed 1 shader contract regression after the sampler-backed 3D branch change, `cargo test -p zircon_runtime --lib lut --locked --jobs 1 --target-dir E:\cargo-targets\zircon-render-main-chain --message-format short --color never` passed 19 LUT-filtered tests, the pre-shaper `cube_lut` run passed 5 cube-LUT tests, `cargo test -p zircon_runtime --lib texture_importer --locked --jobs 1 --target-dir E:\cargo-targets\zircon-render-main-chain --message-format short --color never` passed 14 texture importer tests, `cargo test -p zircon_runtime --lib runtime_diagnostics_combines_core_render_contract_and_missing_externalized_plugins --locked --jobs 1 --target-dir E:\cargo-targets\zircon-render-main-chain --message-format short --color never` passed 1 diagnostics regression, `cargo test -p zircon_runtime --lib render_framework_stats_report_effect_stack_product_node_when_authored --locked --jobs 1 --target-dir E:\cargo-targets\zircon-render-main-chain --message-format short --color never` passed 1 RenderFramework effect-stack stats regression, and `cargo check -p zircon_runtime --lib --locked --jobs 1 --target-dir E:\cargo-targets\zircon-render-main-chain --message-format short --color never` passed with existing warnings. A Windows shaper-guard rerun was blocked before test execution by a target-dir dep-info fingerprint path issue; the isolated WSL rerun `wsl -e sh -lc 'cd /mnt/e/Git/ZirconEngine && CARGO_TARGET_DIR=/tmp/zircon-render-main-chain-cube-lut-0603 cargo test -p zircon_runtime --lib cube_lut --locked --jobs 1 --message-format short --color never'` passed 6 cube-LUT tests, 2500 filtered out, with existing warnings only.
